   301	function labelNear(text: string, at: number, end: number, wanted: Set<string>): string | null {
   302	  const lineStart = text.lastIndexOf("\n", at - 1) + 1;
   303	  let lineEnd = text.indexOf("\n", end);
   304	  if (lineEnd === -1) lineEnd = text.length;
   305	  const wins = [text.slice(Math.max(lineStart, at - WINDOW), at)];
   306	  if (/^\s*\|/.test(text.slice(lineStart, at))) wins.push(text.slice(end, Math.min(lineEnd, end + WINDOW)));
   307	  // ⚠ The previous line counts only when it ENDS in `key:` or `key =`, so the
   308	  // label is the key whose value this line holds. A wider window flagged nonce
   309	  // vectors listed in an array whose NAME contains `sk` (REVIEW-056 adjudication);
   310	  // an array of values under a label is a printed limit instead.
   311	  // ⚠ Blank lines between are skipped: JSON allows any whitespace between a key
   312	  // and its value, and stopping at the first blank line let one hide (R2-1).
   313	  if (lineStart > 0 && /^[\s"'`]*$/.test(text.slice(lineStart, at))) {
   314	    let e = lineStart - 1;
   315	    let prevStart = text.lastIndexOf("\n", e - 1) + 1;
   316	    while (prevStart > 0 && /^\s*$/.test(text.slice(prevStart, e))) { e = prevStart - 1; prevStart = text.lastIndexOf("\n", e - 1) + 1; }
   317	    const m = text.slice(prevStart, e).match(/([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$.\-]*)["'`]?\s*[:=]\s*$/);
   318	    if (m) wins.push(m[1]);
   319	  }
   320	  for (const win of wins) for (const m of win.matchAll(IDENT)) if (components(m[0]).some((c) => wanted.has(c))) return m[0];
   321	  return null;
   322	}
   323	
   324	/**
   325	 * 48 to 66 hex digits, not exactly 64 or 66: a scalar printed without padding
   326	 * drops its leading zero nibbles, one time in sixteen per nibble (`REVIEW-056`
   327	 * S3-1). ⚠ **This was 58 until round two (R2-1)**, which put a valid 57-digit
   328	 * value through. 48 digits means sixteen leading zero nibbles, which a uniform
   329	 * scalar has with probability 2^-64. A label is still required.
   330	 */
   331	const HEX_SCALAR = /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])(?:0x)?([0-9A-Fa-f]{48,66})(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g;
   332	const HEX_32 = /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])(?:0x)?([0-9A-Fa-f]{32})(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g;
   333	
   334	export function labelledHits(text: string): Hit[] {
   335	  const out: Hit[] = [];
   336	  const pub = publicScalars();
   337	  for (const m of text.matchAll(HEX_SCALAR)) {
   338	    const label = labelNear(text, m.index!, m.index! + m[0].length, SECRET_COMPONENTS);
   339	    if (!label) continue;
   340	    const v = m[1].toLowerCase();
   341	    const toy = BigInt("0x" + v) < TOY_SCALAR_BOUND ? "a toy scalar below 2^32" : undefined;
   342	    // One byte repeated is guessable from 256 candidates, the same argument as the seed rule.
   343	    const repeated = v.length % 2 === 0 && v.match(/../g)!.every((b) => b === v.slice(0, 2)) ? "one byte repeated" : undefined;
   344	    out.push({ cls: "labelled-scalar", at: m.index!, len: m[0].length, shown: redact(m[1]), why: `${m[1].length} hex digits labelled '${label}'`, allowedBy: pub.get(v) ?? toy ?? repeated });
   345	  }
   346	  for (const m of text.matchAll(HEX_32)) {
   347	    const label = labelNear(text, m.index!, m.index! + m[0].length, ENTROPY_COMPONENTS);
   348	    if (!label) continue;
   349	    const rule = publicEntropyRule(Uint8Array.from(Buffer.from(m[1], "hex")));
   350	    out.push({ cls: "seed-entropy-hex", at: m.index!, len: m[0].length, shown: redact(m[1]), why: `32 hex digits labelled '${label}'`, allowedBy: rule ? `public entropy: ${rule}` : undefined });
   351	  }
   352	  return out;
   353	}

   355	const ALNUM = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
   356	const rnd = (n: number, alphabet = ALNUM) => Array.from(randomBytes(n), (b) => alphabet[b % alphabet.length]).join("");

   358	/**
   359	 * Each pattern carries a SAMPLE, built here at run time so this file contains
   360	 * none, and `runControls()` plants every sample (`REVIEW-056` S1-1: only two of
   361	 * ten patterns had a control, so deleting any other passed every check).
   362	 */
   363	export const CREDENTIALS: { name: string; re: RegExp; sample: () => string }[] = [
   364	  { name: "PEM private key", re: /-----BEGIN (?:[A-Z0-9]+ )*PRIVATE KEY(?: BLOCK)?-----/g, sample: () => "-----BEGIN " + "RSA PRIVATE KEY-----" },
   365	  { name: "GitHub token", re: /\bgh[pousr]_([A-Za-z0-9]{36})\b/g, sample: () => "gh" + "p_" + rnd(36) },
   366	  { name: "GitHub fine-grained token", re: /\bgithub_pat_([A-Za-z0-9_]{50,})/g, sample: () => "github" + "_pat_" + rnd(60, ALNUM + "_") },
   367	  { name: "Anthropic API key", re: /\bsk-ant-([A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,})/g, sample: () => "sk-" + "ant-" + rnd(40) },
   368	  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\bsk-(?:proj-)?([A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,})/g, sample: () => "sk-" + rnd(48) },
   369	  { name: "AWS access key id", re: /\b(?:AKIA|ASIA)([0-9A-Z]{16})\b/g, sample: () => "AK" + "IA" + rnd(16, "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567") },
   370	  // ⚠ Any spelling of the label, not only `aws_secret_access_key` (R2-2): the AWS
   371	  // CLI's own JSON says `SecretAccessKey`, and config files use hyphens.
   372	  { name: "AWS secret access key", re: /\b(?:aws[_-]?)?secret[_-]?access[_-]?key["'`\s:=]{1,8}([A-Za-z0-9/+]{40})(?![A-Za-z0-9/+])/gi, sample: () => "aws_secret" + "_access_key = " + rnd(40, ALNUM + "/+") },
   373	  { name: "Slack token", re: /\bxox[abprs]-([A-Za-z0-9-]{10,})/g, sample: () => "xo" + "xb-" + rnd(24) },
   374	  // ⚠ Not a trailing `\b`: a key may END in `-`, and `\b` after `-` needs a word
   375	  // character next. Of the 64 characters a key may end in, only `-` was missed, so
   376	  // one key in 64 was invisible. ⚠ **This said one in 32 until round two (R2-11)**:
   377	  // `_` is a word character, so it was never affected. Found as a flaky control.
   378	  { name: "Google API key", re: /\bAIza([0-9A-Za-z_-]{35})(?![0-9A-Za-z_-])/g, sample: () => "AI" + "za" + rnd(35, ALNUM + "_-") },
   379	  { name: "npm token", re: /\bnpm_([A-Za-z0-9]{36})\b/g, sample: () => "np" + "m_" + rnd(36) },
   380	  { name: "GitLab token", re: /\bglpat-([A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,})/g, sample: () => "gl" + "pat-" + rnd(24) },
   381	];
   382	
   383	export function credentialHits(text: string): Hit[] {
   384	  const out: Hit[] = [];
   385	  for (const c of CREDENTIALS) for (const m of text.matchAll(c.re)) {
   386	    // A token whose WHOLE variable part is one character repeated is a placeholder,
   387	    // as in `aws_secret_access_key=AAAA…`. ⚠ **This tested only the last sixteen
   388	    // characters until round two (R2-4)**, so a real token followed by sixteen
   389	    // dashes passed as a placeholder with the real part intact.
   390	    const body = m[1];
   410	  { rule: "Windows home", re: new RegExp(`[A-Za-z]:\\\\{1,2}Users\\\\{1,2}${NAME}`, "g") },
   411	  { rule: "dash-encoded home", re: /(?<![A-Za-z0-9])-(?:Users|home)-([A-Za-z0-9._]+)-/g },
   412	  { rule: "macOS per-user temp", re: new RegExp(`${NOT_URL}(?:/private)?/var/folders/([A-Za-z0-9_+-]{2}/[A-Za-z0-9_+-]{8,})`, "g") },
   413	];
   414	/** User names that identify nobody. Compared case-insensitively. */
   415	export const GENERIC_USERS = new Set(["runner", "user", "username", "you", "yourname", "me", "name", "example", "shared"]);
   416	/**
   417	 * Exact, case-sensitive user names allowed because a cited record uses them as
   418	 * a synthetic fixture. ⚠ **`alice` and `bob` were generic names until round two
   419	 * (R2-5)**, which let a real home directory named alice pass. This is narrower: one spelling,
   420	 * one cited source, and `checkStale` fails if the source no longer carries it.
   421	 */
   422	export const CITED_PATH_NAMES = new Map([["Alice", "REVIEW-056 round one, the leg's synthetic fixture path"]]);
   423	
   424	export function pathHits(text: string): Hit[] {
   425	  const out: Hit[] = [];
   426	  for (const { rule, re } of PATH_RULES) {
   427	    for (const m of text.matchAll(re)) {
   428	      const who = m[1];
   429	      const generic = rule !== "macOS per-user temp" && GENERIC_USERS.has(who.toLowerCase());
   430	      const cited = rule !== "macOS per-user temp" ? CITED_PATH_NAMES.get(who) : undefined;
   431	      out.push({ cls: "personal-path", at: m.index!, len: m[0].length, shown: `${rule}: ${redact(who)}`, why: rule, allowedBy: generic ? "generic user name" : cited });
   432	    }
   433	  }
   434	  return out;
   435	}

    28	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    29	import { defaultStorePaths, loadStore, scanText } from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
    30	
    31	if (import.meta.main) {
    32	  const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
    33	  const write = argv.includes("--write");
    34	  const files = argv.filter((a) => !a.startsWith("--"));
    35	  if (!files.length) { console.log("usage: redact-review-fixtures.ts [--write] docs/reviews/<file>..."); process.exit(2); }
    36	  const outside = files.filter((f) => !f.startsWith("docs/reviews/"));
    37	  if (outside.length) { console.log(`REFUSED: outside docs/reviews/: ${outside.join(", ")}. A secret there is presumed real.`); process.exit(2); }
    38	  const store = loadStore(defaultStorePaths());
    39	  if (!store.secrets) { console.log("REFUSED: no local secret store, so a real secret could not be told from a fixture"); process.exit(2); }
    40	  let total = 0;
    41	  for (const f of files) {
    42	    const text = readFileSync(f, "latin1");
    43	    const hits = scanText(text, store).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy);
    44	    if (hits.some((h) => h.cls === "known-value")) { console.log(`REFUSED: ${f} contains a value from the real secret store. Rotate it; do not redact it.`); process.exit(2); }
    45	    // Replace from the end, skipping any span that overlaps one already replaced.
    46	    const spans = hits.filter((h) => h.len).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
    47	    let out = text, floor = Infinity;
    48	    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    49	    for (const h of spans) {
    50	      if (h.at + h.len! > floor) continue;
    51	      out = out.slice(0, h.at) + `[synthetic ${h.cls} redacted]` + out.slice(h.at + h.len!);
    52	      floor = h.at;
    53	      counts.set(h.cls, (counts.get(h.cls) ?? 0) + 1);
    54	    }
    55	    const left = scanText(out, store).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length;
    56	    const n = [...counts.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    57	    total += n;
    58	    console.log(`${f}: ${[...counts].map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${v}`).join(", ") || "nothing"}; ${left} left`);
    59	    if (write && n) writeFileSync(f, out, "latin1");
    60	    if (left) process.exitCode = 1;
    61	  }
    62	  console.log(`${write ? "redacted" : "would redact"} ${total} span(s)`);
    63	}

What this gate does NOT catch (each is an asserted-uncaught control):
  - a secret split across two string literals or two lines
  - a secret in UTF-16, base64, gzip or any other re-encoding
  - an unlabelled 64-hex ElGamal secret that is NOT in the local store (no shape rule can tell it from a hash)
  - a seed or key in RFC 1751 words, or as a decimal or byte array
  - a home path under a user name on the generic list, or under the one cited fixture name
  - a secret listed as an array element under a label, rather than as the value of a key
  - gitignored files, the reflog and unreachable objects: none of them is published
  - a hostname, a machine serial, or a person's name — only paths and email addresses are personal-data classes
